([hanshuebner/herold](https://github.com/hanshuebner/herold/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/smtp-implementor.md) · ★5)
- **smugan-ai-research**: Researches and saves the guide for one AI engine + model pair that the smugan hook found in the repository without a valid guide. Use only when a smugan message asks for it, passing the pair key and the project path. It confirms the pair in the code, reads official docs and the project's own releases, and saves the guide with `smugan ai-guide --write`. ([DenisCDev/smugan](https://github.com/DenisCDev/smugan/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/smugan-ai-research.md))
- **smuggs**: Smuggs is a ceiling-first NBA DFS decision engine built for small-circle winner-take-all FanDuel contests. Mission: maximize probability of finishing 1st across regular season and playoffs, single-game and multi-game slates, while operating strictly within league research rules. ([mwolff328-stack/WolffClaude](https://github.com/mwolff328-stack/WolffClaude/blob/HEAD/agents/smuggs.md) · ★1)
- **sn-architect**: Plans an AGENT or AGENT-GATED feature in the PLAN phase, after the spec exists and before tasks. Maps each requirement to Fluent primitives, designs the data model and contracts, records risks and the guardrail check, proposes ADRs for HUMAN-DECISION items and removals, splits DECOMPOSE items, writes manual-steps.md, including the HUMAN-BUILD items, and designs spikes for unproven platform facts. ([VitharshanaasreeK25/app_custom_gr](https://github.com/VitharshanaasreeK25/app_custom_gr/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sn-architect.md) · 2 copies)
- **sn-change-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer for a planned ServiceNow change. Use before applying a write — pass it the plan preview the server returned, or an update set — and it checks the target records, the automation and code the change would touch, the policy and the recent history, then returns a go / hold verdict with reasons. It never applies anything. ([IvanBBaev/servicenow-mcp-ai](https://github.com/IvanBBaev/servicenow-mcp-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/sn-change-reviewer.md) · ★13)
- **sn-dev-automation**: Builds Fluent flow, automation and AI agent records (Flow, wfa, Subflow, Action, Playbook, SLA, events, AiAgent, AiAgenticWorkflow, NowAssistSkillConfig) for BUILD tasks tagged dom:automation in an AGENT or AGENT-GATED feature. Use in the BUILD phase only, after SPEC, PLAN and TASKS have exit-checked. ([VitharshanaasreeK25/app_custom_gr](https://github.com/VitharshanaasreeK25/app_custom_gr/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/sn-dev-automation.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **sn-dev-data**: Builds the Fluent data model for BUILD tasks tagged dom:data (Table with typed columns, ChoiceSet, Property, DatabaseView, StateModel and labelled Record seeds). Use in the BUILD phase when tasks.md assigns a data-model task to this agent. ([VitharshanaasreeK25/app_custom_gr](https://github.com/VitharshanaasreeK25/app_custom_gr/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/sn-dev-data.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **sn-dev-integration**: Builds the integration part of one BUILD task, meaning RestMessage, Alias, EmailNotification, InboundEmailAction, ImportSet and GraphqlApi Fluent definitions and their server handler code, with flagged stubs for external systems that have no test counterpart. Use in the BUILD phase for tasks tagged dom:integration (which also covers email and import work), after the spec, plan and tasks are merged. ([VitharshanaasreeK25/app_custom_gr](https://github.com/VitharshanaasreeK25/app_custom_gr/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/sn-dev-integration.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **sn-dev-security**: Builds the access-control layer of a BUILD task tagged dom:security, meaning Role, Acl (one per operation), CrossScopePrivilege (allowed or requested), DataPolicy, UserCriteria and the app permission service. Use in the BUILD phase after the data model exists and before any server or UI task that depends on those permissions. ([VitharshanaasreeK25/app_custom_gr](https://github.com/VitharshanaasreeK25/app_custom_gr/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sn-dev-security.md) · 2 copies)
- **sn-dev-server**: Builds server-side Fluent logic in the BUILD phase for tasks tagged dom:server, covering BusinessRule on app-owned tables only, ScriptInclude, ScheduledScript, RestApi and ScriptAction, plus the shared handler core that REST routes and ATF bridges call. Use it for any tasks.md line tagged [dom:server]. ([VitharshanaasreeK25/app_custom_gr](https://github.com/VitharshanaasreeK25/app_custom_gr/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sn-dev-server.md) · 2 copies)
- **sn-dev-ui**: Builds Fluent UI, catalog and Service Portal artefacts (UiPage React SPA, UiAction, UiPolicy, ClientScript, Form, List, Workspace, Dashboard, Sp records, catalog items) for BUILD tasks tagged dom:ui. Use in the BUILD phase only, when the orchestrator dispatches a task whose tag is dom:ui. ([VitharshanaasreeK25/app_custom_gr](https://github.com/VitharshanaasreeK25/app_custom_gr/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/sn-dev-ui.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **sn-intake-analyst**: Classifies requirements into the intake register during the INTAKE phase, using the sn-intake skill and the assessment workbook method. Use when a requirement list or a workbook csv needs its rows classified and routed before SPEC begins. ([VitharshanaasreeK25/app_custom_gr](https://github.com/VitharshanaasreeK25/app_custom_gr/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/sn-intake-analyst.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **sn-investigator**: Read-only ServiceNow investigator. Use proactively to answer a question about a ServiceNow instance — why a record looks the way it does, what automation runs on a table, where a field or script is used, why a flow failed — without filling the main conversation with raw records. Returns a short report with its evidence. ([IvanBBaev/servicenow-mcp-ai](https://github.com/IvanBBaev/servicenow-mcp-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/sn-investigator.md) · ★13)
- **sn-orchestrator**: Drives one feature through the SDLC pipeline from the output of `node .sdlc/scripts/sdlc.mjs status`, dispatching the next phase agent in order, running each phase exit check, and stopping at every human gate (0, D, 1, 2, 3). Use as the entry point at any phase of any feature, to start, resume or check where a feature stands. - **sn-release-engineer**: Runs the PR step for one feature of a ServiceNow scoped app. Opens every PR kind (intake, decision, design, build, fastlane, scaffold) through sn-pr. For build and fastlane it also bumps the version, runs the local build and checks, writes review.md and the PR body, and appends the manual-step entries builders reported. - **snr-architect**: Principal engineer for system design and structural review. Use to design a new system, plan a significant change, or assess an existing architecture — boundaries, coupling, transaction and validation seams, failure model, one-way doors, and the first scaling bottleneck. Returns a plan or an assessment with ADRs; does not write implementation code. ([Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills](https://github.com/Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/snr-architect.md) · ★3)
- **snr-cloud-auditor**: Cloud, PaaS and container security reviewer. Use for a focused pass over how secrets, identities, networks, containers and infrastructure-as-code limit the damage if a hosting platform, CI or secrets store is breached — returns findings with blast radius, a secrets-ladder table, fixes and verification. Read-only; proposes changes rather than applying them. Defensive security only. ([Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills](https://github.com/Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/snr-cloud-auditor.md) · ★3)
- **snr-data-auditor**: Database and data-layer reviewer. Use for a focused pass over schema, constraints, indexes, query cost, N+1, migrations, transactions, multi-tenancy, backups and data retention/deletion — returning measured findings with plans, fixes and verification. Read-only; never runs a migration or a destructive statement. ([Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills](https://github.com/Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/snr-data-auditor.md) · ★3)
- **snr-incident-responder**: Security incident responder. Use when a provider or platform announces a breach, a credential leaks, a critical runtime CVE is being exploited, or a server may be compromised — returns a classified incident, an ordered rotation plan with verification, a hunt plan per log source and a notification flag. Read-only investigation; proposes actions rather than executing destructive ones. Defensive security only. ([Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills](https://github.com/Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/snr-incident-responder.md) · ★3)
- **snr-pentester**: Authorised penetration tester. Use for a scoped, non-destructive security test of a web app, API or cloud configuration the user owns or has written authorisation to test — builds a risk-ordered test plan, enforces the scope file before any traffic, proves findings with the least harmful evidence and reports each with a fix, a regression test and a detection signal. Refuses to act without a scope. Defensive, authorised testing only. ([Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills](https://github.com/Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/snr-pentester.md) · ★3)
- **snr-php-auditor**: PHP security reviewer. Use for a focused pass over a PHP codebase and its runtime — every PHP version and SAPI in play, status against the October 2026 php2shell fixes (CVE-2026-109618, CVE-2026-109619), php.ini and FPM configuration, PHP-specific code flaws (deserialization, includes, uploads, injection) and Composer, WordPress, Laravel and Symfony hygiene. Read-only; proposes fixes rather than applying them. Defensive security only. ([Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills](https://github.com/Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/snr-php-auditor.md) · ★3)
- **snr-security-auditor**: Application security reviewer. Use for a focused security pass over a codebase or a change — secrets, object-level authorization, injection, authentication, headers, supply chain — returning confirmed findings with locations, reproductions, fixes and regression tests. Read-only; proposes fixes rather than applying them. Defensive security only. ([Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills](https://github.com/Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/snr-security-auditor.md) · ★3)
- **snr-web-auditor**: Public web surface reviewer. Use for a focused pass over SEO, metadata, structured data, 404 and robots, internal linking, accessibility (WCAG 2.2 AA), cookie consent and the trust/conversion layer — returning a per-page findings table with suggested replacement copy. Read-only; a handful of GET requests, no crawling. ([Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills](https://github.com/Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/snr-web-auditor.md) · ★3)

Invoke when user asks to understand code, a file, a function, or a system. ([a-aigner/agentwatch](https://github.com/a-aigner/agentwatch/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/so-explainer.md))
- **so-implementer**: so-pipeline Implement stage. In `slice &lt;id&gt;` mode edits only that slice's files; in `build` mode runs the changed tests (and a build only when asked) and fixes errors within plan scope. The orchestrator always sets the model per dispatch. ([rknall/steward](https://github.com/rknall/steward/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/so-implementer.md) · ★1)
- **so-investigator**: so-pipeline Investigation stage. Clarifies a task against the steward codebase and writes the task explanation. Dispatched only by the so-pipeline orchestrator. ([rknall/steward](https://github.com/rknall/steward/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/so-investigator.md) · ★1)
- **so-performance**: Performance analyst. Identifies runtime bottlenecks, N+1 queries, inefficient algorithms, and memory issues. Invoke when user reports slowness, timeouts, or high resource usage. Read-only — produces a report, does not fix code. ([a-aigner/agentwatch](https://github.com/a-aigner/agentwatch/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/so-performance.md))
- **so-plan-reviewer**: so-pipeline Plan Review stage. Verifies the plan absorbs every investigation, refactor and review finding and that slices are disjoint and well-tiered; emits implementation waves or replan. Dispatched only by the so-pipeline orchestrator. ([rknall/steward](https://github.com/rknall/steward/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/so-plan-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **so-planner**: so-pipeline Plan stage. Turns the investigation into a sliced plan with disjoint file sets, acceptance criteria, model tiers and waves; reworks it from findings. Dispatched only by the so-pipeline orchestrator. ([rknall/steward](https://github.com/rknall/steward/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/so-planner.md) · ★1)
- **so-planner**: Requirements analyst and SPEC writer. Use when starting any new feature or task. Invoke when user requests a new feature, says "만들어줘", "개발해줘", "plan", "spec", or starts a new task. ([a-aigner/agentwatch](https://github.com/a-aigner/agentwatch/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/so-planner.md))
- **so-preflight**: Pre-merge safety checker. Runs tests, checks for lint errors, reviews for obvious security issues, and confirms the branch is ready to merge or deploy. Invoke automatically before PR creation or when user asks if code is ready. ([a-aigner/agentwatch](https://github.com/a-aigner/agentwatch/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/so-preflight.md))
- **so-presenter**: so-pipeline Present stage. Writes PRESENT.md for the user covering what changed, what to watch for and how to test it in the Adobe AIR TSO client. Dispatched only by the so-pipeline orchestrator. ([rknall/steward](https://github.com/rknall/steward/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/so-presenter.md) · ★1)
- **so-quality**: Code quality enforcer. Reviews and refactors passing code without changing behavior (TDD REFACTOR phase). Invoke after all tests pass. Also invoked by /simplify command. ([a-aigner/agentwatch](https://github.com/a-aigner/agentwatch/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/so-quality.md))
- **so-refactorer**: so-pipeline Refactor stage. Read-only audit of the task's diff for duplication, missed reuse and shadowed sources of truth; routes to review or replan. Never edits. Dispatched only by the so-pipeline orchestrator (sonnet, opus for large tasks). ([rknall/steward](https://github.com/rknall/steward/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/so-refactorer.md) · ★1)
- **so-responder**: Stack Overflow agent — triages node.js, php and python questions, decides between answering, adding to an existing answer or skipping, drafts and posts to the owner's account after approval. Use for any request involving Stack Overflow in this project. ([caioagiani/stackoverflow-agent](https://github.com/caioagiani/stackoverflow-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/so-responder.md))
- **so-reviewer**: so-pipeline Review stage. Verifies every plan item against the code with file:line evidence, runs the changed tests, reads the Draft PR's CI result, and routes to present, reimplement or replan. Dispatched only by the so-pipeline orchestrator. ([rknall/steward](https://github.com/rknall/steward/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/so-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **so-reviewer**: Critical SPEC evaluator. Use after Planner to validate requirements before any implementation. Always pauses for user confirmation before proceeding. ([a-aigner/agentwatch](https://github.com/a-aigner/agentwatch/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/so-reviewer.md))
- **so-security**: Security reviewer. Performs deep security analysis on code changes — OWASP Top 10, authentication/authorization logic, injection risks, secrets exposure, and dependency vulnerabilities. Invoke when user asks for security review, or automatically before PR creation on security-sensitive changes. ([a-aigner/agentwatch](https://github.com/a-aigner/agentwatch/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/so-security.md))
- **so-tester**: Test-first engineer. Writes tests before implementation (TDD RED phase). Also verifies tests pass after implementation (TDD GREEN verification). Invoke before developer for RED phase, or after developer to verify GREEN phase. ([a-aigner/agentwatch](https://github.com/a-aigner/agentwatch/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/so-tester.md))
- **so-what-auditor**: The skeptic on data-analysis discover. Rates one candidate pattern for materiality against the decision's baseline and for the claim level its evidence supports, and names the next check that would settle it. ([kuan51/claude-skills](https://github.com/kuan51/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/data-analysis/agents/so-what-auditor.md))
- **SOA**: The Synthetic Operator Agent — an empirical, read-only virtual laboratory technician and student machine operator. SOA interacts with machine digital twins strictly through the live Three.js / browser GUI, testing buttons, knobs, dials, screen visibility, kinematic collisions, and ergonomic slope angles. ([SREphoto/Twins](https://github.com/SREphoto/Twins/blob/HEAD/.master/02_AGENT_WORKFORCE/agents/SOA.md))
- **soak-analyst**: Use to analyse one soak or memory time series: least-squares slope with its 95% bound, settled baseline, recycle and instrument-health checks, delta-M against the budget, and what the run does and does not establish. Never edits source. ([waffle-commons/monorepo](https://github.com/waffle-commons/monorepo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/soak-analyst.md) · ★1)
- **soak-engineer**: Owns the M2 link-loss resilience soak (docs/specs/m2-soak-fuzz.md). Invoke for soak/fuzz test work, retry/backoff bounds, heartbeat-gate behavior under link drop, and nightly slow-suite changes. ([ianshank/GCP-Drone-Comms-Unit](https://github.com/ianshank/GCP-Drone-Comms-Unit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/soak-engineer.md) · ★1)
- **soak-test-engineer**: owns long-duration stability tests. invoke when before launches. ([Imdevsup/game_builder_claude](https://github.com/Imdevsup/game_builder_claude/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/soak-test-engineer.md))

- **soap-downloader**: Development specialist for the MNI SOAP client in pje-download. Use when modifying PJe court communication or download logic. &lt;example&gt; Context: User reports download failures from a specific court user: "Downloads from TJES are timing out on the SOAP call" assistant: "I'll dispatch the SOAP downloader specialist to investigate mni_client." &lt;commentary&gt;SOAP timeout issues need MNI-specific debugging.&lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([fbmoulin/pje-download](https://github.com/fbmoulin/pje-download/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/soap-downloader.md) · ★8)
- **soap-porter**: Ports a single SOAP operation to a full hexagonal stack — domain model, repository trait + implementation, service trait + implementation, thin Tauri command, and TypeScript wrapper. ([pongsathonwon/pos-drug-front](https://github.com/pongsathonwon/pos-drug-front/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/soap-porter.md))
- **soar-analyst**: Specialized in deep SOAR case and approval investigation - cross-references case history, the full audit trail, and threat-intel verdicts before recommending a decision on an ambiguous pending approval, or when a case review spans multiple related cases. Invoke for complex/ambiguous approval reviews, not simple single-case lookups (use the investigate-case skill for those directly). ([varunmaggo/SOAR](https://github.com/varunmaggo/SOAR/blob/HEAD/plugin/soar-ops/agents/soar-analyst.md))
- **soar-architect**: Use this agent when choosing a SOAR platform, deciding which security workflows to automate, or designing the orchestration that connects SIEM, EDR, email security, identity, and ticketing tools. It produces the automation strategy, playbook designs with human approval gates, enrichment flows, and MTTR and ROI metrics, leaving implementation to automation-builder. ([Ormus-Solutions/LibreSecOps-Claude-Code](https://github.com/Ormus-Solutions/LibreSecOps-Claude-Code/blob/HEAD/plugins/security-automation/agents/soar-architect.md) · ★5)
- **soat-an-toan-cong-truong**: Gác cổng an toàn và thẩm quyền - soát văn bản công trường: rủi ro an toàn bị bỏ sót, câu đổ lỗi, tự kết luận đạt, tự tính kết cấu, quy chuẩn không dặn kiểm tra hiện hành. - **socratic**: Interviews the user relentlessly about a plan or design until reaching shared understanding, resolving each branch of the decision tree. Use when user wants to stress-test a plan or get grilled on their design ([daleal/dotfiles](https://github.com/daleal/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/dotfiles/layers/base/user/.config/opencode/agents/socratic.md) · ★1)
- **socratic**: Reformulates a fuzzy brainstorming topic into a precise problem statement with How Might We variants, constraints, and out-of-scope items. Phase 0 of the brainstorm pipeline. ([giordanorec/claude-brainstorm-multiagent](https://github.com/giordanorec/claude-brainstorm-multiagent/blob/HEAD/agents/socratic.md) · ★1)
- **socratic**: Socratic Agent Nabu (= агент #20 конвейера синтеза Nabu). Задаёт уточняющие вопросы вокруг свежей записи (clarify/challenge/extend). Активируется ТОЛЬКО по явному вызову пользователя или конфигурации. Жёсткий лимит: ≤5 вопросов, ≤1 challenging, без моральной оценки. Private/vault обрабатываются локально. ([NoeticEcho/Nabu-claude](https://github.com/NoeticEcho/Nabu-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/socratic.md) · ★1)
- **Socratic Mentor**: Pedagogical tutor that teaches through Socratic questioning — never provides direct code answers, builds understanding through guided inquiry. ([slashwhy/super-todo](https://github.com/slashwhy/super-todo/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/socratic-mentor.agent.md) · ★5)
- **Socratic Oracle**: Provide spoiler-free guidance. ([rasmniel/dotfiles](https://github.com/rasmniel/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.config/opencode/agents/socratic-oracle.md))
- **socratic-challenger**: Socratic design challenger. Asks probing, sometimes naive-sounding questions to expose gaps, hidden assumptions, and missed simpler alternatives in RFCs and designs. Use after drafting an RFC or design to stress-test it through dialogue before implementation. ([kookr-ai/kookr](https://github.com/kookr-ai/kookr/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/socratic-challenger.md) · ★5)
- **socratic-challenger**: Challenges an idea, plan or decision with Socratic questions on demand, optionally summarizes the decisions reached and keeps a Q&A log, and never implements features. Requires the socratic-method skill. ([JSisques/shitaku](https://github.com/JSisques/shitaku/blob/HEAD/website/src/content/docs/es/catalog/agents/socratic-challenger.md) · 3 copies)
- **socratic-duel-participant**: Participates in a Socratic Duel run using pre-resolved launcher context and participant-owned artifact writes. ([rp1-run/rp1](https://github.com/rp1-run/rp1/blob/HEAD/plugins/base/agents/socratic-duel-participant.md) · ★38)
- **socratic-examiner**: Fresh-memory assessment specialist. Use to author a small batch of knowledge-check questions for a concept, or to grade a learner's free-text ("in your own words") answers against a rubric — unbiased, because it has no stake in the lesson. Grades a BATCH in one call, never one subagent per question. ([dibakshya01/dronAIcharya](https://github.com/dibakshya01/dronAIcharya/blob/HEAD/agents/socratic-examiner.md))
- **socratic-guide**: Question-driven problem resolution through guided discovery methodology. Builds debugging intuition and systematic thinking skills through strategic questioning. Use PROACTIVELY for learning-oriented problem solving. ([dotclaude/marketplace](https://github.com/dotclaude/marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/adaptive-learning/agents/socratic-guide.md) · ★43)
- **socratic-interviewer**: You are an expert requirements engineer conducting a Socratic interview to clarify vague ideas into actionable requirements. ([Q00/ouroboros](https://github.com/Q00/ouroboros/blob/HEAD/src/ouroboros/agents/socratic-interviewer.md) · ★6.4k · 6 copies)
- **socratic-interviewer**: Expert at conducting Socratic interviews to clarify vault setup requirements — asks focused questions, never builds ([hongdangmoo49/oh-my-obsidian](https://github.com/hongdangmoo49/oh-my-obsidian/blob/HEAD/agents/socratic-interviewer.md) · ★31)
- **socratic-interviewer**: You are an expert requirements engineer conducting a Socratic interview to clarify vague ideas into actionable specifications. ([baeseokjae/paveda](https://github.com/baeseokjae/paveda/blob/HEAD/assets/harness/skills/specify/agents/socratic-interviewer.md))
- **socratic-mentor**: Educational guide specializing in Socratic method for programming knowledge with focus on discovery learning through strategic questioning ([SuperClaude-Org/SuperClaude_Framework](https://github.com/SuperClaude-Org/SuperClaude_Framework/blob/HEAD/plugins/superclaude/agents/socratic-mentor.md) · ★24k · 40 copies)
- **socratic-mentor**: Use when you need an educational guide specializing in the Socratic method for programming knowledge, focusing on discovery learning rather than direct answers. ([derHaken/SuperAntigravity](https://github.com/derHaken/SuperAntigravity/blob/HEAD/agents/socratic-mentor.md) · ★437)
- **socratic-mentor**: Optional module — teaching subagent that builds understanding by questioning instead of answering: locates the learner, leads with the difficulty the idea resolves, reveals the idea in one sentence, and shows the full answer last and minimally. Use when the learner wants to LEARN a concept rather than be handed it. Explains and questions; never edits files. ([arielybarak/agent-lab](https://github.com/arielybarak/agent-lab/blob/HEAD/course-factory/course-template/.claude/agents/socratic-mentor.md))
- **socratic-mentor**: Educational guide specializing in Socratic method for programming knowledge with focus on discovery learning through strategic questioning. Ideal for mentoring sessions, concept reinforcement, and reflective learning journeys. ([NickCrew/Claude-Cortex](https://github.com/NickCrew/Claude-Cortex/blob/HEAD/archive/agents/socratic-mentor.md))
- **socratic-mentor**: Guides paper authors through Socratic questions to sharpen arguments and surface unstated assumptions ([TheAxeC/ordo](https://github.com/TheAxeC/ordo/blob/HEAD/skills/academic/paper/agents/socratic_mentor_agent.md))
- **socratic-mentor** ([W-nova/ars-research-suite-dsh](https://github.com/W-nova/ars-research-suite-dsh/blob/HEAD/agents/socratic-mentor.md))
- **socratic-note-coach**: Use this agent when you have a fleeting thought, a literature note, or a raw idea that you want to transform into a high-quality, atomic permanent note for your Zettelkasten. This agent acts as a Socratic mentor, asking probing questions to help you clarify your thinking and find connections. &lt;example&gt;Context: The user has a rough idea about cognitive load. user: "I want to write a note about how too many tabs open reduces focus." assistant: "I will use the socratic-note-coach to help refine this into a permanent note." &lt;commentary&gt;The user has a raw idea that needs to be developed into a per… ([RaulCD3597/dotfiles](https://github.com/RaulCD3597/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/opencode/.config/opencode/agents/socratic-note-coach.md))
- **socratic-orchestrator**: 소크라테스식 Q&A 루프를 관리하고 Thinker-Questioner 간 컨텍스트를 영속적으로 유지 ([chkim-su/philosopher](https://github.com/chkim-su/philosopher/blob/HEAD/agents/socratic-orchestrator.md) · ★1)
- **socratic-partner**: Deep design dialogue partner for Socratic questioning sessions. Responds with structured 5-part answers and never agrees without evidence. ([gihwan-dev/claude-setup](https://github.com/gihwan-dev/claude-setup/blob/HEAD/agents/socratic-partner.md))
- **socratic-probe-writer**: Write one to three short Socratic probe questions for a single transformed synthetic scar and return only JSON. ([hedges333/makeflow-skill](https://github.com/hedges333/makeflow-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/socratic-probe-writer.md))
- **socratic-questioner**: 품질 기반 만족 조건으로 질문하는 brainstorm 역할 에이전트 ([chkim-su/philosopher](https://github.com/chkim-su/philosopher/blob/HEAD/agents/socratic-questioner.md) · ★1)
- **socratic-questioner**: Use this agent to generate sharp, specific Socratic questions that move a learner forward without revealing the answer. Specialized in turning vague stuckness into concrete next steps the learner can attempt. Never produces solutions — only questions, pointers, and challenges. ([trusted-american/marketplace](https://github.com/trusted-american/marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/ensenador/agents/socratic-questioner.md))
- **socratic-questioner**: 苏格拉底追问者问清源。5轮×5问结构化澄清（宏观→中观→微观→验证→收敛），信息完整度四维评估（背景/目标/约束/成功标准，取最低分），隐含假设密度识别与风险分级。用于研究任务信息不足时的强制澄清。触发词：澄清、追问、信息不足、需求模糊、帮我问清楚、完整度评估、假设识别、研究想法还不成熟、只说了个方向。 ([virologist/research-orchestration-team](https://github.com/virologist/research-orchestration-team/blob/HEAD/agents/socratic-questioner.md))
- **socratic-questioning**: Migrated from `.agents/skills/reasoning/socratic-questioning/SKILL.md` (v1.0.0) into the single-file `reasoning-strategy/` convention. ([PMCRO-AI-Agent-Company/AgentSkills.Template](https://github.com/PMCRO-AI-Agent-Company/AgentSkills.Template/blob/HEAD/plugins/pmcro-omode/agents/socratic-questioning.md))
- **socratic-reviewer**: Reviews a document's argument, not its facts — do the claims support the conclusions, did the author obey the owner's directives, was the author skeptical of its own sources. Forest, not trees. ([rnortman/claude-plugins](https://github.com/rnortman/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/review-chain/agents/socratic-reviewer.md))
